FILE /tmp/wixcli/node_modules/@wix/cli/build/DevCommand-AWQ5PZA6.js LINE 660
          forcedJSONParsing: false,
          clarifyTimeoutError: false
        },
        disableWixHeaders: true
      });
      if (text === "") {
        return {
          manifest: null,
          isTunnelRequired: false
        };
      }
      if (devMetadata != null) {
        const { staticsUrlPlaceholder, serverUrlPlaceholder } = devMetadata;
        const placeholders = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
        placeholders.set(staticsUrlPlaceholder, baseUrl);
        placeholders.set(serverUrlPlaceholder, tunnelUrl);
        return {
          manifest: parseLooseAppManifest(text, placeholders),
          isTunnelRequired: text.includes(serverUrlPlaceholder)
        };
      }
      const manifestV1 = parseLooseAppManifest(text);
      return {
        manifest: manifestV1,
        isTunnelRequired: isTunnelRequired(manifestV1)
      };
    },
    [base, http]
  );
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/DevCommand/utils/DevCenterWatcher.ts
init_esm_shims();
import EventEmitter from "node:events";
var DevCenterWatcher = class extends EventEmitter {
  onManifestUpdated(listener) {
    this.on("manifestUpdated", listener);
    return () => {
      this.off("manifestUpdated", listener);
    };
  }
};

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/DevCommand/hooks/checkManifestForChanges.ts
init_esm_shims();
var import_variant = __toESM(require_lib(), 1);
import { isDeepStrictEqual } from "node:util";
var ManifestUpdateResult = (0, import_variant.variant)({
  Changed: (0, import_variant.fields)(),
  Unchanged: {}
});
function checkManifestForChanges(previous, latest) {
  if (previous == null) {
    return ManifestUpdateResult.Changed({ updatedComponents: [] });
  }
  if (isDeepStrictEqual(previous, latest)) {
    return ManifestUpdateResult.Unchanged();
  }
  const oldComponentsMap = new Map(
    previous.components.map((comp) => [comp.compId, comp])
  );
  const changedComponents = latest.components.filter((newComp) => {
    const oldComp = oldComponentsMap.get(newComp.compId);
    return !isDeepStrictEqual(newComp, oldComp);
  });
  return ManifestUpdateResult.Changed({ updatedComponents: changedComponents });
}

var import_variant = __toESM(require_lib(), 1);
import { isDeepStrictEqual } from "node:util";
var ManifestUpdateResult = (0, import_variant.variant)({
  Changed: (0, import_variant.fields)(),
  Unchanged: {}
});
function checkManifestForChanges(previous, latest) {
  if (previous == null) {
    return ManifestUpdateResult.Changed({ updatedComponents: [] });
  }
  if (isDeepStrictEqual(previous, latest)) {
    return ManifestUpdateResult.Unchanged();
  }
  const oldComponentsMap = new Map(
    previous.components.map((comp) => [comp.compId, comp])
  );
  const changedComponents = latest.components.filter((newComp) => {
    const oldComp = oldComponentsMap.get(newComp.compId);
    return !isDeepStrictEqual(newComp, oldComp);
  });
  return ManifestUpdateResult.Changed({ updatedComponents: changedComponents });
}

var { pollInterval } = getTestOverrides();
var { devCenterDraftOverrides } = createEnv();
var overrideLifecycleState = devCenterDraftOverrides ? LifecycleState.DRAFT : void 0;
var useDevCenterUpdater = ({
  siteId,
  base,
  serverLocalUrl,
  serverExposedUrl,
  devMetadata
}) => {
  const devCenterWatcher = (0, import_react4.useMemo)(() => new DevCenterWatcher(), []);
  const [versionOverrideId, setVersionOverrideId] = (0, import_react4.useState)();
  const [manifest, setManifest] = (0, import_react4.useState)(null);
  const { reportError } = useErrorReporter();
  const writeToDebugLog = useDebugLog();
  const exit = useExit();
  const {
    model: { projectFolder, releaseAppId }
  } = useProjectModel();
  const logger = useAstroLogger({ isPrefixed: true });
  const devCenterOverrides = useDevCenterOverridesClient({ siteId });
  const openTunnel = useTunnel();
  const tunnelUrl = (0, import_react4.useRef)();
  const [tunnelRequired, setTunnelRequired] = (0, import_react4.useState)(false);
  const fetchAppManifest = useFetchAppManifest({ base });
  const baseUrl = serverExposedUrl ?? serverLocalUrl;
  const updateDevCenter = (0, import_react4.useCallback)(
    async (latestManifest, isTunnelRequired2) => {
      const manifestUpdateResult = checkManifestForChanges(
        manifest,
        latestManifest
      );
      if ((0, import_variant2.isType)(manifestUpdateResult, "Unchanged")) {
        return manifestUpdateResult;
      }
      if (!serverExposedUrl && !tunnelUrl.current && isTunnelRequired2) {
        const config = await readUserConfig();
        if (!config.tunneling) {
          setTunnelRequired(true);
          return ManifestUpdateResult.Unchanged();
        }
        const serverLocalPort = parseInt(new URL(serverLocalUrl).port, 10);
        const tunnel = await openTunnel(serverLocalPort);
        tunnelUrl.current = tunnel.url;
        return ManifestUpdateResult.Unchanged();
      }
      const appConfig = await readAppConfig(projectFolder, writeToDebugLog);
      let updatedVersionOverrideId = appConfig?.devArmTag;
      if (!updatedVersionOverrideId) {
        updatedVersionOverrideId = await devCenterOverrides.createComponentsOverride({
          type: OverrideType.DEV,
          appManifest: latestManifest,
          projectId: releaseAppId,
          lifecycleState: overrideLifecycleState
        });
      } else {
        const updateState = await devCenterOverrides.updateComponentsOverrides({
          id: updatedVersionOverrideId,
          type: OverrideType.DEV,
          appManifest: latestManifest,
          projectId: releaseAppId
        });
        if (!(0, import_variant2.isType)(updateState, "Updated")) {
          updatedVersionOverrideId = await devCenterOverrides.createComponentsOverride({
            type: OverrideType.DEV,
            appManifest: latestManifest,
            projectId: releaseAppId,
            lifecycleState: overrideLifecycleState
          });
        }
      }
      if (updatedVersionOverrideId !== appConfig?.devArmTag) {
        await saveAppConfig(projectFolder, {
          devArmTag: updatedVersionOverrideId
        });
      }
      setManifest(latestManifest);
      setVersionOverrideId(updatedVersionOverrideId);
      devCenterWatcher.emit("manifestUpdated", {
        versionOverrideId: updatedVersionOverrideId,
        updatedComponents: manifestUpdateResult.updatedComponents
      });
      return manifestUpdateResult;
    },
    [
      releaseAppId,
      manifest,
      serverLocalUrl,
      serverExposedUrl,
      devCenterOverrides,
      projectFolder,
      openTunnel,
      devCenterWatcher,
      writeToDebugLog
    ]
  );
  const [timer, setTimer] = (0, import_react4.useState)(Date.now());
  (0, import_react4.useEffect)(() => {
    let disposed = false;
    async function fetchAndUpdate() {
      const { manifest: manifest2, isTunnelRequired: isTunnelRequired2 } = await fetchAppManifest(
        serverLocalUrl,
        devMetadata,
        baseUrl,
        tunnelUrl.current ?? baseUrl
      );
      if (manifest2 == null) {
        return;
      }
      try {
        const result = await updateDevCenter(manifest2, isTunnelRequired2);
        if ((0, import_variant2.isType)(result, "Changed")) {
          logger.log(
            "App manifest updated. Refresh existing browser tabs to view the latest changes."
          );
          if (process.env.REMOTE_MACHINE_TASK_EVENTS_URL) {
            const eventData = {
              type: "manifest_update",
              appId: manifest2.appId,
              overrideId: versionOverrideId,
              result: "SUCCESS"
            };
            void fetch(process.env.REMOTE_MACHINE_TASK_EVENTS_URL, {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify({
                eventName: "manifest-update",
                taskId: process.env.REMOTE_MACHINE_TASK_ID,
                executionId: process.env.REMOTE_MACHINE_EXECUTION_ID,
                timestamp: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(),
                eventData
              })
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (error) {
        setManifest(manifest2);
        logger.error(/* @__PURE__ */ import_react4.default.createElement(ErrorViewer, { error }));
        writeToDebugLog(error);
        reportError(error);
        if (process.env.REMOTE_MACHINE_TASK_EVENTS_URL) {
          const normalizedError = normalizeError(error);
          const httpError = extractHttpError(normalizedError);
          if (httpError) {
            const errorInfo = getHttpErrorInfo(httpError);
            let validationViolations;
            try {
              if (isHttpValidationError(httpError)) {
                validationViolations = httpError.response?.data.details.validationError.fieldViolations.map(
                  (violation) => `${violation.field}: ${violation.description}`
                ).join("\n");
              }
            } catch {
            }
            const eventData = {
              type: "manifest_update",
              appId: manifest2.appId,
              overrideId: versionOverrideId ?? "",
              result: "FAILURE",
              code: errorInfo.response.status,
              message: errorInfo.response.data.message,
              validationViolations
            };
            void fetch(process.env.REMOTE_MACHINE_TASK_EVENTS_URL, {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              body: JSON.stringify({
                eventName: "manifest-update",
                taskId: process.env.REMOTE_MACHINE_TASK_ID,
                executionId: process.env.REMOTE_MACHINE_EXECUTION_ID,
                timestamp: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(),
                eventData
              })
            });
          }
        }
        throw error;
      }
    }
    void fetchAndUpdate().catch((error) => {
      if (error instanceof Error && error.name === "TrustedAppIsNotMarkedAsInternal") {
        exit(error);
      }
    }).finally(() => {
      if (!disposed) {
        setTimeout(() => setTimer(Date.now()), pollInterval ?? 1e3);
      }
    });
    return () => {
      disposed = true;
    };
  }, [timer]);
  return { baseUrl, tunnelRequired, versionOverrideId, devCenterWatcher };
};

  children
}) => {

      return SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId: userDefinedSiteId });
    }
    const siteId = (await readAppConfig(projectFolder, writeDebugLog))?.siteId;
    if (!siteId && (process2.stdin.isTTY !== true || process2.stdout.isTTY !== true)) {
      throw new CliError({
        code: CliErrorCode.DevelopmentSiteNotConfigured(),
        cause: null
      });
    }

    async (siteId) => {

        throw new Error("Cannot change site: siteId is user-defined");

        await saveAppConfig(projectFolder, { siteId });
        merge({ result: SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId }) });
      } catch (e) {
        throw new CliError({
          code: CliErrorCode.FailedToSaveDevelopmentSite({
            path: ASTRO_APP_CONFIG_PATH
          }),
          cause: e
        });

    [exit, setSiteId]
  );

    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(Spinner, { text: "Resolving development site..." }),
    Success: ({ result: state }) => {
      return (0, import_variant6.match)(state, {

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/DevCommand/app-flow/site/SiteProvider.tsx
var SiteProvider = ({
  children
}) => {
  const exit = useExit();
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  const { projectFolder, env } = model;
  const userDefinedSiteId = env.WIX_SITE_ID;
  const writeDebugLog = useDebugLog();
  const { status, merge } = useAsync(async () => {
    if (userDefinedSiteId) {
      return SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId: userDefinedSiteId });
    }
    const siteId = (await readAppConfig(projectFolder, writeDebugLog))?.siteId;
    if (!siteId && (process2.stdin.isTTY !== true || process2.stdout.isTTY !== true)) {
      throw new CliError({
        code: CliErrorCode.DevelopmentSiteNotConfigured(),
        cause: null
      });
    }
    return siteId ? SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId }) : SiteValue.NotConfigured();
  }, []);
  const setSiteId = (0, import_react12.useCallback)(
    async (siteId) => {
      if (userDefinedSiteId) {
        throw new Error("Cannot change site: siteId is user-defined");
      }
      try {
        await saveAppConfig(projectFolder, { siteId });
        merge({ result: SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId }) });
      } catch (e) {
        throw new CliError({
          code: CliErrorCode.FailedToSaveDevelopmentSite({
            path: ASTRO_APP_CONFIG_PATH
          }),
          cause: e
        });
      }
    },
    [merge, projectFolder, userDefinedSiteId]
  );
  const handleResolveSite = (0, import_react12.useCallback)(
    (siteId) => setSiteId(siteId).catch(exit),
    [exit, setSiteId]
  );
  return (0, import_variant6.match)(status, {
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(Spinner, { text: "Resolving development site..." }),
    Success: ({ result: state }) => {
      return (0, import_variant6.match)(state, {
        NotConfigured: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(ResolveSite, { onSubmit: handleResolveSite }),
        Resolved: ({ siteId }) => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(
          SiteContext.Provider,
          {
            value: {
              siteId,
              setSiteId,

=====

FILE /tmp/wixcli/node_modules/@wix/cli/build/DevCommand-AWQ5PZA6.js LINE 901
              })
            });
          }
        }
        throw error;
      }
    }
    void fetchAndUpdate().catch((error) => {
      if (error instanceof Error && error.name === "TrustedAppIsNotMarkedAsInternal") {
        exit(error);
      }
    }).finally(() => {
      if (!disposed) {
        setTimeout(() => setTimer(Date.now()), pollInterval ?? 1e3);
      }
    });
    return () => {
      disposed = true;
    };
  }, [timer]);
  return { baseUrl, tunnelRequired, versionOverrideId, devCenterWatcher };
};

var TunnelingConsent = ({ required, children }) => {
  const exit = useExit();
  const [approved, setApproved] = (0, import_react5.useState)();
  const { status } = useAsync(async () => {
    const config = await readUserConfig();
    if (config.tunneling) {
      setApproved(true);
    }
    return config;
  }, []);
  if (!required || approved) {
    return children;
  }
  return /* @__PURE__ */ import_react5.default.createElement(import_react5.default.Fragment, null, (0, import_variant3.match)(status, {
    Success: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react5.default.createElement(
      ConfirmInput,
      {
        label: "To continue, a secure tunnel will be set up from your machine for development purposes.",
        initialValue: true,
        onSubmit: async (approved2) => {
          setApproved(approved2);
          await updateUserConfig({ tunneling: approved2 });
          if (!approved2) {
            exit();
          }
        }
      }
    ),
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => null
  }), approved === false && /* @__PURE__ */ import_react5.default.createElement(Text, { skin: "error" }, "Operation aborted. Tunneling is necessary to run your application."));
};

function useSiteDeploymentTopologyUpdater() {
  const { baseUrl, versionOverrideId } = useDevEnvironment();
  const { siteId } = useSiteConfig();
  const updateTopology = useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId);
  const exit = useExit();
  (0, import_react7.useEffect)(() => {
    updateTopology({
      versionOverrideId,
      siteUrl: baseUrl,
      environment: "development"
    }).catch(exit);
  }, [versionOverrideId, baseUrl, updateTopology, exit]);
}

var SiteDev = () => {
  const { appId, siteId } = useSiteConfig();
  const { baseUrl, versionOverrideId } = useDevEnvironment();
  const veloClient = useVeloClient();
  const links = useSiteLinks({
    cmd: "dev",
    siteId,
    overrideId: versionOverrideId,
    siteUrl: baseUrl
  });
  const { status: gitHubOnboardingStatus } = useAsync(
    () => veloClient.getGitHubOnboardingStatus(appId),
    [appId]
  );
  useSiteDeploymentTopologyUpdater();
  const isGitHubEnabled = (0, import_variant4.match)(gitHubOnboardingStatus, {
    Success: ({ result }) => result?.status === OnboardingState.GITHUB_ENABLED,
    Error: () => false,
    Loading: () => false
  });
  const visibleLinks = isGitHubEnabled ? links : links.filter((link) => link.platform !== "editor");

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/DevCommand/app-flow/site/SiteProvider.tsx
var SiteProvider = ({
  children
}) => {
  const exit = useExit();
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  const { projectFolder, env } = model;
  const userDefinedSiteId = env.WIX_SITE_ID;
  const writeDebugLog = useDebugLog();
  const { status, merge } = useAsync(async () => {
    if (userDefinedSiteId) {
      return SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId: userDefinedSiteId });
    }
    const siteId = (await readAppConfig(projectFolder, writeDebugLog))?.siteId;
    if (!siteId && (process2.stdin.isTTY !== true || process2.stdout.isTTY !== true)) {
      throw new CliError({
        code: CliErrorCode.DevelopmentSiteNotConfigured(),
        cause: null
      });
    }
    return siteId ? SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId }) : SiteValue.NotConfigured();
  }, []);
  const setSiteId = (0, import_react12.useCallback)(
    async (siteId) => {
      if (userDefinedSiteId) {
        throw new Error("Cannot change site: siteId is user-defined");
      }
      try {
        await saveAppConfig(projectFolder, { siteId });
        merge({ result: SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId }) });
      } catch (e) {
        throw new CliError({
          code: CliErrorCode.FailedToSaveDevelopmentSite({
            path: ASTRO_APP_CONFIG_PATH
          }),
          cause: e
        });
      }
    },
    [merge, projectFolder, userDefinedSiteId]
  );
  const handleResolveSite = (0, import_react12.useCallback)(
    (siteId) => setSiteId(siteId).catch(exit),
    [exit, setSiteId]
  );
  return (0, import_variant6.match)(status, {
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(Spinner, { text: "Resolving development site..." }),
    Success: ({ result: state }) => {
      return (0, import_variant6.match)(state, {
        NotConfigured: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(ResolveSite, { onSubmit: handleResolveSite }),
        Resolved: ({ siteId }) => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(
          SiteContext.Provider,
          {
            value: {
              siteId,
              setSiteId,
              canChangeSite: !userDefinedSiteId
            }
          },
          children(siteId)
        )
      });
    }
  });
};

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/DevCommand/app-flow/site/SiteProvider.tsx
var SiteProvider = ({
  children
}) => {
  const exit = useExit();
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  const { projectFolder, env } = model;
  const userDefinedSiteId = env.WIX_SITE_ID;
  const writeDebugLog = useDebugLog();
  const { status, merge } = useAsync(async () => {
    if (userDefinedSiteId) {
      return SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId: userDefinedSiteId });
    }
    const siteId = (await readAppConfig(projectFolder, writeDebugLog))?.siteId;
    if (!siteId && (process2.stdin.isTTY !== true || process2.stdout.isTTY !== true)) {
      throw new CliError({
        code: CliErrorCode.DevelopmentSiteNotConfigured(),
        cause: null
      });
    }
    return siteId ? SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId }) : SiteValue.NotConfigured();
  }, []);
  const setSiteId = (0, import_react12.useCallback)(
    async (siteId) => {
      if (userDefinedSiteId) {
        throw new Error("Cannot change site: siteId is user-defined");
      }
      try {
        await saveAppConfig(projectFolder, { siteId });
        merge({ result: SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId }) });
      } catch (e) {
        throw new CliError({
          code: CliErrorCode.FailedToSaveDevelopmentSite({
            path: ASTRO_APP_CONFIG_PATH
          }),
          cause: e
        });
      }
    },
    [merge, projectFolder, userDefinedSiteId]
  );
  const handleResolveSite = (0, import_react12.useCallback)(
    (siteId) => setSiteId(siteId).catch(exit),
    [exit, setSiteId]
  );
  return (0, import_variant6.match)(status, {
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(Spinner, { text: "Resolving development site..." }),
    Success: ({ result: state }) => {
      return (0, import_variant6.match)(state, {
        NotConfigured: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(ResolveSite, { onSubmit: handleResolveSite }),
        Resolved: ({ siteId }) => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(
          SiteContext.Provider,
          {
            value: {
              siteId,
              setSiteId,
              canChangeSite: !userDefinedSiteId
            }
          },
          children(siteId)
        )
      });
    }
  });
};

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/DevCommand/app-flow/site/SiteProvider.tsx
var SiteProvider = ({
  children
}) => {
  const exit = useExit();
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  const { projectFolder, env } = model;
  const userDefinedSiteId = env.WIX_SITE_ID;
  const writeDebugLog = useDebugLog();
  const { status, merge } = useAsync(async () => {
    if (userDefinedSiteId) {
      return SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId: userDefinedSiteId });
    }
    const siteId = (await readAppConfig(projectFolder, writeDebugLog))?.siteId;
    if (!siteId && (process2.stdin.isTTY !== true || process2.stdout.isTTY !== true)) {
      throw new CliError({
        code: CliErrorCode.DevelopmentSiteNotConfigured(),
        cause: null
      });
    }
    return siteId ? SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId }) : SiteValue.NotConfigured();
  }, []);
  const setSiteId = (0, import_react12.useCallback)(
    async (siteId) => {
      if (userDefinedSiteId) {
        throw new Error("Cannot change site: siteId is user-defined");
      }
      try {
        await saveAppConfig(projectFolder, { siteId });
        merge({ result: SiteValue.Resolved({ siteId }) });
      } catch (e) {
        throw new CliError({
          code: CliErrorCode.FailedToSaveDevelopmentSite({
            path: ASTRO_APP_CONFIG_PATH
          }),
          cause: e
        });
      }
    },
    [merge, projectFolder, userDefinedSiteId]
  );
  const handleResolveSite = (0, import_react12.useCallback)(
    (siteId) => setSiteId(siteId).catch(exit),
    [exit, setSiteId]
  );
  return (0, import_variant6.match)(status, {
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(Spinner, { text: "Resolving development site..." }),
    Success: ({ result: state }) => {
      return (0, import_variant6.match)(state, {
        NotConfigured: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(ResolveSite, { onSubmit: handleResolveSite }),
        Resolved: ({ siteId }) => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react12.default.createElement(
          SiteContext.Provider,
          {
            value: {
              siteId,
              setSiteId,
              canChangeSite: !userDefinedSiteId
            }
          },
          children(siteId)
        )
      });
    }
  });
};

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/DevCommand/utils/runAstroDev.ts
init_esm_shims();
var EVENT_LINE = "line";
var EVENT_SERVER_ADDRESS = "server_address";
function runAstroDev({
  packageManager,
  projectFolder,
  port,
  allowedHosts
}) {
  const binaryArgs = ["astro", "dev"];
  if (port) {
    binaryArgs.push("--port", `${port}`);
  }
  if (allowedHosts) {
    binaryArgs.push("--allowed-hosts", allowedHosts);
  }
  const { file, args } = packageManager.getRunBinaryCmd(binaryArgs);
  const result = execa(file, args, {
    cwd: projectFolder,
    stdio: "pipe",
    env: { FORCE_COLOR: "1" },
    cleanup: true,
    windowsHide: false
  });
  let stdoutBuffer = "";
  result.stdout?.on("data", (chunk) => {
    stdoutBuffer += chunk.toString();
    const lines = stdoutBuffer.split("\n");
    stdoutBuffer = lines.pop() ?? "";
    for (const line of lines) {
      result.stdout?.emit(EVENT_LINE, line);
      if (line.includes("http://")) {
        const match6 = /(http:\/\/[^\s]+)/.exec(line);
        if (match6?.[1]) {
          const address = stripAnsi(match6[1]).trim();
          result.stdout?.emit(EVENT_SERVER_ADDRESS, address);
        }
      }
    }
  });
  let stdErrBuffer = "";
  result.stderr?.on("data", (chunk) => {
    stdErrBuffer += chunk.toString();
    const lines = stdErrBuffer.split("\n");
    stdErrBuffer = lines.pop() ?? "";
    for (const line of lines) {
      result.stderr?.emit(EVENT_LINE, line);
    }
  });
  return result;
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/DevCommand/utils/startDevServer.ts
async function startDevServer({
  model: { pluginsInterface, projectFolder },
  port,
  allowedHosts,
  logger,
  onUnexpectedExit,
  base
}) {
  if (await isStandaloneExtensionsProject(projectFolder)) {
    return startStandaloneExtensionsDevServer({
      projectFolder,
      port,
      allowedHosts,
      base,
      pluginsInterface
    });
  }
  return startAstroDevServer({
    projectFolder,
    port,
    allowedHosts,
    logger,
    onUnexpectedExit
  });
}
async function startStandaloneExtensionsDevServer({
  projectFolder,
  port,
  allowedHosts,
  base,
  pluginsInterface
}) {
  const { dev } = await importExtensionsPackageNodeApi(projectFolder);
  const onRuntimeError = pluginsInterface != null && pluginsInterface.eventsEmitter.listenerCount("runtime-error") > 0 ? (event) => {
    void pluginsInterface.eventsEmitter.emit("runtime-error", event);
  } : void 0;
  const server = await dev({
    rootDir: projectFolder,
    base,
    onRuntimeError,
    port,
    allowedHosts: allowedHosts?.split(",").map((host) => host.trim())
  });
  return {
    ready: Promise.resolve(`http://localhost:${server.address.port}`),
    stop: () => server.stop().catch(() => {
    })
  };
}
async function startAstroDevServer({
  projectFolder,
  port,
  allowedHosts,
  logger,
  onUnexpectedExit
}) {
  const repoType = await getRepoType(projectFolder);
  const packageManager = await createPackageManager(repoType);
  const astro = runAstroDev({
    packageManager,
    projectFolder,
    port,
    allowedHosts
  });
  const ready = new Promise((resolve) => {
    astro.stdout?.once(EVENT_SERVER_ADDRESS, (address) => {
      resolve(address);
    });
  });
  astro.stdout?.on(EVENT_LINE, (line) => logger.log(line));
  astro.stderr?.on(EVENT_LINE, (line) => logger.error(line));
  let stoppedByUs = false;
  let cleanedUp = false;

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/DevCommand/utils/runAstroDev.ts
init_esm_shims();
var EVENT_LINE = "line";
var EVENT_SERVER_ADDRESS = "server_address";
function runAstroDev({
  packageManager,
  projectFolder,
  port,
  allowedHosts
}) {
  const binaryArgs = ["astro", "dev"];
  if (port) {
    binaryArgs.push("--port", `${port}`);
  }
  if (allowedHosts) {
    binaryArgs.push("--allowed-hosts", allowedHosts);
  }
  const { file, args } = packageManager.getRunBinaryCmd(binaryArgs);
  const result = execa(file, args, {
    cwd: projectFolder,
    stdio: "pipe",
    env: { FORCE_COLOR: "1" },
    cleanup: true,
    windowsHide: false
  });
  let stdoutBuffer = "";
  result.stdout?.on("data", (chunk) => {
    stdoutBuffer += chunk.toString();
    const lines = stdoutBuffer.split("\n");
    stdoutBuffer = lines.pop() ?? "";
    for (const line of lines) {
      result.stdout?.emit(EVENT_LINE, line);
      if (line.includes("http://")) {
        const match6 = /(http:\/\/[^\s]+)/.exec(line);
        if (match6?.[1]) {
          const address = stripAnsi(match6[1]).trim();
          result.stdout?.emit(EVENT_SERVER_ADDRESS, address);
        }
      }
    }
  });
  let stdErrBuffer = "";
  result.stderr?.on("data", (chunk) => {
    stdErrBuffer += chunk.toString();
    const lines = stdErrBuffer.split("\n");
    stdErrBuffer = lines.pop() ?? "";
    for (const line of lines) {
      result.stderr?.emit(EVENT_LINE, line);
    }
  });
  return result;
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/DevCommand/utils/startDevServer.ts
async function startDevServer({
  model: { pluginsInterface, projectFolder },
  port,
  allowedHosts,
  logger,
  onUnexpectedExit,
  base
}) {
  if (await isStandaloneExtensionsProject(projectFolder)) {
    return startStandaloneExtensionsDevServer({
      projectFolder,
      port,
      allowedHosts,
      base,
      pluginsInterface
    });
  }
  return startAstroDevServer({
    projectFolder,
    port,
    allowedHosts,
    logger,
    onUnexpectedExit
  });
}
async function startStandaloneExtensionsDevServer({
  projectFolder,
  port,
  allowedHosts,
  base,
  pluginsInterface
}) {
  const { dev } = await importExtensionsPackageNodeApi(projectFolder);
  const onRuntimeError = pluginsInterface != null && pluginsInterface.eventsEmitter.listenerCount("runtime-error") > 0 ? (event) => {
    void pluginsInterface.eventsEmitter.emit("runtime-error", event);
  } : void 0;
  const server = await dev({
    rootDir: projectFolder,
    base,
    onRuntimeError,
    port,
    allowedHosts: allowedHosts?.split(",").map((host) => host.trim())
  });
  return {
    ready: Promise.resolve(`http://localhost:${server.address.port}`),
    stop: () => server.stop().catch(() => {
    })
  };
}
async function startAstroDevServer({
  projectFolder,
  port,
  allowedHosts,
  logger,
  onUnexpectedExit
}) {
  const repoType = await getRepoType(projectFolder);
  const packageManager = await createPackageManager(repoType);
  const astro = runAstroDev({
    packageManager,
    projectFolder,
    port,
    allowedHosts
  });
  const ready = new Promise((resolve) => {
    astro.stdout?.once(EVENT_SERVER_ADDRESS, (address) => {
      resolve(address);
    });
  });
  astro.stdout?.on(EVENT_LINE, (line) => logger.log(line));
  astro.stderr?.on(EVENT_LINE, (line) => logger.error(line));
  let stoppedByUs = false;
  let cleanedUp = false;

      return;
    }

// ../arm-client/src/isTrustedInternalErr.ts
init_esm_shims();
function isTrustedInternalErr(e) {
  if (isHttpError(e)) {
    const appError = e.response?.data?.details?.applicationError;
    if (appError?.description) {
      let errDescription;
      try {
        errDescription = JSON.parse(appError.description);
      } catch {
      }
      return errDescription?.results?.some(
        (result) => result.error?.startsWith("scriptAsset is not supported")
      );
    }
  }
  return false;
}

var { AppPermissionDenied } = CliErrorCode;
var UpdateAppReleaseData = (0, import_variant.variant)({
  TagNotFound: (0, import_variant.fields)(),
  TagUpdated: {}
});
var ARMClient = class {
  constructor(httpClient) {
    this.httpClient = httpClient;
    const { minRetryTimeout, retries } = getTestOverrides();
    this.retryOptions = {
      maxTimeout: 3 * 1e3,
      minTimeout: minRetryTimeout,
      retries: retries ?? 3
    };
  }
  retryOptions;
  createComponents = async (appId, componentConfigs, projectId) => {
    try {
      await this.httpClient.request(
        createComponents({ appId, componentConfigs, projectId })
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (isHttpError(e) && e.response?.status === 403) {
        throw new CliError({
          cause: e,
          code: AppPermissionDenied({ appId })
        });
      }
      throw new CliError({
        cause: e,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToCreateComponents()
      });
    }
  };
  createAppRelease = async (manifest, projectId) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await pRetry(
        () => this.httpClient.request(
          createAppRelease({
            appId: manifest.appId,
            componentConfigs: manifest.components,
            projectId
          })
        ),
        this.retryOptions
      );
      return createAppReleaseSchema.parse(data).tag;
    } catch (e) {
      if (isHttpError(e)) {
        if (e.response?.status === 403) {
          throw new CliError({
            cause: e,
            code: AppPermissionDenied({ appId: manifest.appId })
          });
        }
        if (e.response?.status === 400 && isHttpValidationError(e)) {
          throw new CliError({
            cause: e,
            code: CliErrorCode.AppReleaseConfigViolations()
          });
        }
      }
      if (isTrustedInternalErr(e)) {
        throw new CliError({
          cause: e,
          code: CliErrorCode.TrustedAppIsNotMarkedAsInternal()
        });
      }
      throw new CliError({
        cause: e,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToCreateAppRelease()
      });
    }
  };
  updateAppRelease = async (manifest, tag) => {
    try {
      await pRetry(
        () => this.httpClient.request(
          updateAppRelease({
            appId: manifest.appId,
            componentConfigs: manifest.components,
            tag
          })
        ),
        this.retryOptions
      );
      return UpdateAppReleaseData.TagUpdated();
    } catch (e) {
      if (isHttpError(e)) {
        if (e.response?.status === 404) {
          return UpdateAppReleaseData.TagNotFound({ cause: e });
        }
        if (e.response?.status === 403) {
          throw new CliError({
            cause: e,
            code: AppPermissionDenied({ appId: manifest.appId })
          });
        }
        if (e.response?.status === 400 && isHttpValidationError(e)) {
          throw new CliError({
            cause: e,
            code: CliErrorCode.AppReleaseConfigViolations()
          });
        }
      }
      if (isTrustedInternalErr(e)) {
        throw new CliError({
          cause: e,
          code: CliErrorCode.TrustedAppIsNotMarkedAsInternal()
        });
      }
      throw new CliError({
        cause: e,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToUpdateAppRelease()
      });
    }
  };

            componentConfigs: manifest.components,
            tag
          })
        ),
        this.retryOptions

    } catch (e) {
      if (isHttpError(e)) {
        if (e.response?.status === 404) {
          return UpdateAppReleaseData.TagNotFound({ cause: e });

        if (e.response?.status === 403) {
          throw new CliError({
            cause: e,
            code: AppPermissionDenied({ appId: manifest.appId })
          });

            cause: e,
            code: CliErrorCode.AppReleaseConfigViolations()

        throw new CliError({

  createDraftVersion = async (appId, tag) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        commitComponentOverride({
          appId,
          componentOverrideId: tag
        })
      );
      return commitComponentOverrideSchema.parse(data);
    } catch (cause) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToCreateDraftVersion()
      });
    }
  };
  releaseDraftVersion = async (appId, options) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        promoteAppVersionFromDraftToProduction({
          appId,
          createNewMajor: options.createNewMajor,
          latestMajor: options.latestMajor,
          notes: options.notes
        })
      );
      const { fullVersion } = promoteAppVersionFromDraftToProductionSchema.parse(data);
      return { releasedVersion: fullVersion };
    } catch (cause) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToReleaseDraftVersion()
      });
    }
  };
  releaseHeadlessProject = async ({
    appId,
    comment,
    componentOverrideId,
    createMinorVersion
  }) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        release({
          appId,
          componentOverrideId,
          createMinorVersion,
          notes: comment
        })
      );
      return releaseSchema.parse(data).releaseBaseUrl;
    } catch (e) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause: e,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToReleaseHeadlessProject()
      });
    }
  };
};

var import_react = __toESM(require_react(), 1);
function useARMClient() {
  const httpClient = useHttpClient({ type: "backoffice" });
  const client = (0, import_react.useMemo)(() => new ARMClient(httpClient), [httpClient]);
  return usePanoramaMethodLogger(client);
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/ReleaseCommand/ReleaseSite.tsx
var ReleaseSite = ({
  baseUrl,
  label,
  createMinorVersion,
  comment
}) => {
  const { appId, siteId } = useSiteConfig();
  const { getAppProject } = useBackendAsAServiceClient();
  const { releaseHeadlessProject } = useARMClient();
  const updateDeploymentTopology = useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId);
  const releaseAction = useAsyncCallback(
    async (_, previewId, previewUrl) => {
      const deploymentBaseUrl = await releaseHeadlessProject({
        appId,
        componentOverrideId: previewId,
        createMinorVersion,
        comment
      });
      await updateDeploymentTopology({
        environment: "production",
        siteUrl: previewUrl ?? "",
        versionOverrideId: previewId
      });
      const appProject = await getAppProject(appId);
      return { baseUrl: appProject?.customDomain?.domain ?? deploymentBaseUrl };
    }
  );
  const { uploadStatus } = useCreatePreview({
    baseUrl,
    label,

FILE /tmp/wixcli/node_modules/@wix/cli/build/ReleaseCommand-5PK4GS32.js LINE 705
    try {
      await pRetry(
        () => this.httpClient.request(
          updateAppRelease({
            appId: manifest.appId,
            componentConfigs: manifest.components,
            tag
          })
        ),
        this.retryOptions
      );
      return UpdateAppReleaseData.TagUpdated();
    } catch (e) {
      if (isHttpError(e)) {
        if (e.response?.status === 404) {
          return UpdateAppReleaseData.TagNotFound({ cause: e });
        }
        if (e.response?.status === 403) {
          throw new CliError({
            cause: e,
            code: AppPermissionDenied({ appId: manifest.appId })
          });
        }
        if (e.response?.status === 400 && isHttpValidationError(e)) {
          throw new CliError({
            cause: e,
            code: CliErrorCode.AppReleaseConfigViolations()
          });
        }
      }
      if (isTrustedInternalErr(e)) {
        throw new CliError({
          cause: e,
          code: CliErrorCode.TrustedAppIsNotMarkedAsInternal()
        });
      }
      throw new CliError({
        cause: e,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToUpdateAppRelease()
      });
    }

  createDraftVersion = async (appId, tag) => {

          appId,
          componentOverrideId: tag
        })
      );
      return commitComponentOverrideSchema.parse(data);
    } catch (cause) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToCreateDraftVersion()
      });
    }
  };
  releaseDraftVersion = async (appId, options) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        promoteAppVersionFromDraftToProduction({
          appId,
          createNewMajor: options.createNewMajor,
          latestMajor: options.latestMajor,
          notes: options.notes
        })
      );
      const { fullVersion } = promoteAppVersionFromDraftToProductionSchema.parse(data);
      return { releasedVersion: fullVersion };
    } catch (cause) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToReleaseDraftVersion()
      });
    }
  };
  releaseHeadlessProject = async ({
    appId,
    comment,
    componentOverrideId,
    createMinorVersion
  }) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        release({
          appId,
          componentOverrideId,
          createMinorVersion,
          notes: comment
        })
      );
      return releaseSchema.parse(data).releaseBaseUrl;
    } catch (e) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause: e,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToReleaseHeadlessProject()
      });
    }
  };

function useARMClient() {

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/ReleaseCommand/ReleaseSite.tsx
var ReleaseSite = ({
  baseUrl,
  label,
  createMinorVersion,
  comment
}) => {
  const { appId, siteId } = useSiteConfig();
  const { getAppProject } = useBackendAsAServiceClient();
  const { releaseHeadlessProject } = useARMClient();
  const updateDeploymentTopology = useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId);
  const releaseAction = useAsyncCallback(
    async (_, previewId, previewUrl) => {
      const deploymentBaseUrl = await releaseHeadlessProject({
        appId,
        componentOverrideId: previewId,
        createMinorVersion,
        comment
      });
      await updateDeploymentTopology({
        environment: "production",
        siteUrl: previewUrl ?? "",
        versionOverrideId: previewId
      });
      const appProject = await getAppProject(appId);
      return { baseUrl: appProject?.customDomain?.domain ?? deploymentBaseUrl };
    }
  );
  const { uploadStatus } = useCreatePreview({
    baseUrl,
    label,
    onPreviewCreated: ({ previewId, deployment: { deploymentUrl } }) => {
      void releaseAction.execute(previewId, deploymentUrl);
    }
  });
  return /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(import_react2.default.Fragment, null, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(
    ActionStatus,
    {
      status: uploadStatus,
      loadingText: "Uploading your application files...",
      successText: "Uploaded successfully!"
    }
  ), (0, import_variant2.match)(releaseAction.status, {
    NotRequested: () => null,
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Spinner, { text: /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Text, null, "Releasing...") }),
    Success: ({ result: { baseUrl: baseUrl2 } }) => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Box_default, { flexDirection: "column", gap: 1 }, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Alert, { type: "success" }, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Text, { bold: true }, "Site published on ", baseUrl2)))
  }));
};

  releaseDraftVersion = async (appId, options) => {

      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        promoteAppVersionFromDraftToProduction({
          appId,
          createNewMajor: options.createNewMajor,
          latestMajor: options.latestMajor,
          notes: options.notes

      });
    }

    appId,
    comment,
    componentOverrideId,
    createMinorVersion
  }) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        release({
          appId,
          componentOverrideId,
          createMinorVersion,
          notes: comment
        })
      );
      return releaseSchema.parse(data).releaseBaseUrl;
    } catch (e) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause: e,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToReleaseHeadlessProject()
      });
    }
  };

// ../arm-client/src/useARMClient.ts

var import_react = __toESM(require_react(), 1);
function useARMClient() {
  const httpClient = useHttpClient({ type: "backoffice" });
  const client = (0, import_react.useMemo)(() => new ARMClient(httpClient), [httpClient]);
  return usePanoramaMethodLogger(client);
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/ReleaseCommand/ReleaseSite.tsx
var ReleaseSite = ({
  baseUrl,
  label,
  createMinorVersion,
  comment
}) => {
  const { appId, siteId } = useSiteConfig();
  const { getAppProject } = useBackendAsAServiceClient();
  const { releaseHeadlessProject } = useARMClient();
  const updateDeploymentTopology = useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId);
  const releaseAction = useAsyncCallback(
    async (_, previewId, previewUrl) => {
      const deploymentBaseUrl = await releaseHeadlessProject({
        appId,
        componentOverrideId: previewId,
        createMinorVersion,
        comment
      });
      await updateDeploymentTopology({
        environment: "production",
        siteUrl: previewUrl ?? "",
        versionOverrideId: previewId
      });
      const appProject = await getAppProject(appId);
      return { baseUrl: appProject?.customDomain?.domain ?? deploymentBaseUrl };
    }
  );
  const { uploadStatus } = useCreatePreview({
    baseUrl,
    label,
    onPreviewCreated: ({ previewId, deployment: { deploymentUrl } }) => {
      void releaseAction.execute(previewId, deploymentUrl);
    }
  });
  return /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(import_react2.default.Fragment, null, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(
    ActionStatus,
    {
      status: uploadStatus,
      loadingText: "Uploading your application files...",
      successText: "Uploaded successfully!"
    }
  ), (0, import_variant2.match)(releaseAction.status, {
    NotRequested: () => null,
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Spinner, { text: /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Text, null, "Releasing...") }),
    Success: ({ result: { baseUrl: baseUrl2 } }) => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Box_default, { flexDirection: "column", gap: 1 }, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Alert, { type: "success" }, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Text, { bold: true }, "Site published on ", baseUrl2)))
  }));
};

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/ReleaseCommand/ReleaseSite.tsx
var ReleaseSite = ({
  baseUrl,
  label,
  createMinorVersion,
  comment
}) => {
  const { appId, siteId } = useSiteConfig();
  const { getAppProject } = useBackendAsAServiceClient();
  const { releaseHeadlessProject } = useARMClient();
  const updateDeploymentTopology = useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId);
  const releaseAction = useAsyncCallback(
    async (_, previewId, previewUrl) => {
      const deploymentBaseUrl = await releaseHeadlessProject({
        appId,
        componentOverrideId: previewId,
        createMinorVersion,
        comment
      });
      await updateDeploymentTopology({
        environment: "production",
        siteUrl: previewUrl ?? "",
        versionOverrideId: previewId
      });
      const appProject = await getAppProject(appId);
      return { baseUrl: appProject?.customDomain?.domain ?? deploymentBaseUrl };
    }
  );
  const { uploadStatus } = useCreatePreview({
    baseUrl,
    label,
    onPreviewCreated: ({ previewId, deployment: { deploymentUrl } }) => {
      void releaseAction.execute(previewId, deploymentUrl);
    }
  });
  return /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(import_react2.default.Fragment, null, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(
    ActionStatus,
    {
      status: uploadStatus,
      loadingText: "Uploading your application files...",
      successText: "Uploaded successfully!"
    }
  ), (0, import_variant2.match)(releaseAction.status, {
    NotRequested: () => null,
    Error: () => null,

// ../../node_modules/@wix/anyide-document-deployment/dist/src/index.js
var deployDocument = async (document, options) => {
  const { branchId, revision, hashes } = await getBase(options.httpClient);
  const { httpClient, onStatusChange, pollInterval: pollInterval2, timeout } = options;
  const documentUpdate = filterDocumentPagesByHashes(hashes, document);
  if (Object.keys(documentUpdate.pages).length === 0) {
    return {
      branchId,
      revision
    };
  }
  const updateSiteTaskId = await updateSite(httpClient, documentUpdate, branchId, revision);
  return subscribeToUpdateSiteTaskStatus(httpClient, updateSiteTaskId, { onStatusChange, pollInterval: pollInterval2, timeout });
};

// ../cli-site/src/components/PreviewCommand/useDeploySitePreview.ts
init_esm_shims();
var import_react = __toESM(require_react(), 1);
function useDeploySitePreview() {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  const { deploySitePreview } = useVeloClient();
  return (0, import_react.useCallback)(
    async ({ branchId, revision, code }) => {
      const rootFiles = await loadProjectRootFiles(model.projectFolder);
      return deploySitePreview({
        deploymentData: {
          branchId,
          revision: String(revision),
          content: {
            ...code,
            files: code.files.concat(rootFiles),
            layout: GridAppLayout[code.layout]
          }
        }
      });
    },
    [deploySitePreview, model.projectFolder]
  );
}

// ../cli-site/src/components/PreviewCommand/useCreatePreview.ts
var { pollInterval } = getTestOverrides();
function toSiteUrl(deploymentUrl) {
  const url = new URL(deploymentUrl);
  url.search = "";
  return url.toString();
}
function useCreatePreview() {
  const dmClient = useDocumentManagementClient();
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  const httpClient = useHttpClient({ type: "editor" });
  const deploySitePreview = useDeploySitePreview();
  return (0, import_react2.useCallback)(async () => {
    const siteFsManager = await createSiteFsManager(model.projectFolder);
    const [code, document] = await Promise.all([
      siteFsManager.getCode(),
      siteFsManager.getDocument()
    ]);
    const { branchId, revision } = await deployDocument(document, {
      httpClient,
      pollInterval
    }).catch((error) => {
      throw new CliError({
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToDeployDocument(),
        cause: error
      });
    });
    const preview = await deploySitePreview({ branchId, revision, code });
    return {
      deploymentId: preview.deploymentId,
      previewUrl: preview.deploymentShortUrl ?? preview.deploymentUrl,
      siteUrl: toSiteUrl(preview.deploymentUrl),
      deploymentStatus: preview.isPublishPipelineDeployment ? await dmClient.getDeploymentDetails(preview.deploymentId) : DeploymentStatus.Success()
    };
  }, [model.projectFolder, httpClient, deploySitePreview, dmClient]);
}

  useCreatePreview
};

// ../dev-center-overrides-client/src/utils.ts
var { AppPermissionDenied } = CliErrorCode;
function toModifiedComponents(components) {
  return components.map((comp) => ({
    componentId: comp.compId,
    data: comp.compData,
    name: comp.compName,
    type: comp.compType,
    createdBy: comp.createdBy,
    createdByVersion: comp.createdByVersion
  }));
}
function toComponentExperiments(components) {
  return components.flatMap(
    (comp) => comp.experiments?.enabledBy ? [
      {
        componentId: comp.compId,
        spec: comp.experiments.enabledBy,
        variantValue: "true",
        experimentActionType: ExperimentActionType.CREATE_COMPONENT,
        createComponentOptions: { experimentVersion: 1 }
      }
    ] : []
  );
}
function handleReleaseError(e, appId, fallbackCode) {
  if (isHttpError(e)) {
    if (e.response?.status === 403) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause: e,
        code: AppPermissionDenied({ appId })
      });
    }
    if (e.response?.status === 400 && isHttpValidationError(e)) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause: e,
        code: CliErrorCode.AppReleaseConfigViolations()
      });
    }
  }
  if (isTrustedInternalErr(e)) {
    throw new CliError({
      cause: e,
      code: CliErrorCode.TrustedAppIsNotMarkedAsInternal()
    });
  }
  throw new CliError({ cause: e, code: fallbackCode });
}

// ../dev-center-overrides-client/src/client.ts
var UpdateComponentsOverridesData = (0, import_variant.variant)({
  NotFound: (0, import_variant.fields)(),
  Updated: {}
});
var DevCenterOverridesClient = class {
  constructor(httpClient) {
    this.httpClient = httpClient;
  }
  getAppLatestVersion = async (appId) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        getLatestProductionVersion({ appId })
      );
      const { appVersion } = getLatestProductionVersionResponseSchema.parse(data);
      return appVersion?.version ?? 0;
    } catch (e) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause: e,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToFetchLatestProductionVersion()
      });
    }
  };
  createComponentsOverride = async ({
    type,
    appManifest: { appId, components },
    lifecycleState,
    projectId,
    experiments
  }) => {
    try {
      const appVersion = await this.getAppLatestVersion(appId);
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        createComponentsOverride({
          componentsOverride: {
            id: randomUUID(),
            type,
            appId,
            appVersion,
            lifecycleState,
            externalId: projectId,
            modifiedComponents: toModifiedComponents(components),
            experiments
          }
        })
      );
      return overrideIdResponseSchema.parse(data).componentsOverride.id;
    } catch (e) {
      handleReleaseError(e, appId, CliErrorCode.FailedToCreateAppRelease());
    }
  };
  updateComponentsOverrides = async ({
    id,
    type,
    appManifest: { appId, components },
    projectId
  }) => {
    try {
      const appVersion = await this.getAppLatestVersion(appId);
      await this.httpClient.request(
        updateComponentsOverrides({
          componentsOverride: {
            id,
            type,
            appId,
            appVersion,
            externalId: projectId,
            modifiedComponents: toModifiedComponents(components)
          },
          fieldMask: ["type", "appVersion", "modifiedComponents"]
        })
      );
      return UpdateComponentsOverridesData.Updated();
    } catch (e) {
      if (isHttpError(e) && e.response?.status === 404) {
        return UpdateComponentsOverridesData.NotFound({ cause: e });
      }
      handleReleaseError(e, appId, CliErrorCode.FailedToUpdateAppRelease());
    }
  };
  updateOverrideWithNoSideEffects = async ({
    id,
    appManifest: { appId, components },
    componentFieldMasks,
    projectId
  }) => {
    try {
      await this.httpClient.request(
        updateOverrideWithNoSideEffects({
          componentsOverride: {
            id,
            appId,
            externalId: projectId,
            modifiedComponents: toModifiedComponents(components)
          },
          componentFieldMasks
        })
      );
      return id;
    } catch (e) {
      handleReleaseError(
        e,
        appId,
        CliErrorCode.FailedToUpdateOverrideWithNoSideEffects()
      );
    }
  };
  createDraftVersion = async (appId, componentsOverrideId) => {
    try {
      await this.httpClient.request(
        commitOverride({ componentsOverrideId, onlyEmitEvent: false })
      );
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        suggestNextVersions({ appId })
      );
      const { nextMajor, nextMinor } = suggestNextVersionsResponseSchema.parse(data);
      return { nextMajor, nextMinor };
    } catch (cause) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToCreateDraftVersion()
      });
    }
  };
  releaseDraftVersion = async (appId, options) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        promoteAppVersionFromDraftToProduction({
          appId,
          createNewMajor: options.createNewMajor,
          notes: options.notes,
          promoteStrategy: PromoteStrategy.ENTIRE_APP,
          version: options.latestMajor
        })
      );
      const { appVersion } = promoteAppVersionFromDraftResponseSchema.parse(data);
      return { releasedVersion: appVersion.fullVersion ?? "" };
    } catch (cause) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToReleaseDraftVersion()
      });
    }
  };
};

// ../dev-center-overrides-client/src/useDevCenterOverridesClient.ts
function useDevCenterOverridesClient({
  siteId
} = {}) {
  const httpClient = useHttpClient({ type: "backoffice", siteId });
  const client = (0, import_react.useMemo)(
    () => new DevCenterOverridesClient(httpClient),
    [httpClient]
  );
  return usePanoramaMethodLogger(client);
}

  return { platform, label: DEFAULT_PLATFORM_LABELS[platform], url };
}
function resolvePlatformLinks(model, input, links) {
  if (model.pluginsInterface === void 0) {
    return links;
  }
  return model.pluginsInterface.resolvePlatformLinks({
    ...input,
    appId: model.config.appId,
    links
  });
}

    defaultLink("site", siteUrl),
    defaultLink("dashboard", getSiteDashboardUrl(siteId, overrideId)),

      );
      const { nextMajor, nextMinor } = suggestNextVersionsResponseSchema.parse(data);
      return { nextMajor, nextMinor };
    } catch (cause) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToCreateDraftVersion()
      });
    }
  };
  releaseDraftVersion = async (appId, options) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        promoteAppVersionFromDraftToProduction({
          appId,
          createNewMajor: options.createNewMajor,
          notes: options.notes,
          promoteStrategy: PromoteStrategy.ENTIRE_APP,
          version: options.latestMajor
        })
      );
      const { appVersion } = promoteAppVersionFromDraftResponseSchema.parse(data);
      return { releasedVersion: appVersion.fullVersion ?? "" };
    } catch (cause) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToReleaseDraftVersion()
      });
    }
  };
};

        updateComponentsOverrides({
          componentsOverride: {
            id,
            type,
            appId,
            appVersion,
            externalId: projectId,
            modifiedComponents: toModifiedComponents(components)
          },
          fieldMask: ["type", "appVersion", "modifiedComponents"]
        })

      return UpdateComponentsOverridesData.Updated();

        return UpdateComponentsOverridesData.NotFound({ cause: e });
      }
      handleReleaseError(e, appId, CliErrorCode.FailedToUpdateAppRelease());
    }

function toTopologyLink(links, platform) {
  const link = links.find((link2) => link2.platform === platform);
  if (link === void 0) {
    return;
  }
  return { main: link.url };
}
function useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react3.useCallback)(
    async ({
      environment,
      siteUrl,
      versionOverrideId
    }) => {
      const links = resolveSiteLinks(model, {
        cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId,
        siteUrl
      });
      await writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(
        model.projectFolder,
        environment,
        {
          id: versionOverrideId,
          site: toTopologyLink(links, "site"),
          dashboard: toTopologyLink(links, "dashboard")
        }
      );
    },
    [model, siteId]
  );
}
function useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForApp(siteId) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react3.useCallback)(
    async ({
      environment,
      versionOverrideId
    }) => {
      const links = resolveAppLinks(model, {
        cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId
      });
      await writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(
        model.projectFolder,
        environment,
        {
          id: versionOverrideId,
          site: toTopologyLink(links, "site"),

        }

  );

  };
  releaseDraftVersion = async (appId, options) => {
    try {
      const { data } = await this.httpClient.request(
        promoteAppVersionFromDraftToProduction({
          appId,
          createNewMajor: options.createNewMajor,
          notes: options.notes,
          promoteStrategy: PromoteStrategy.ENTIRE_APP,
          version: options.latestMajor
        })
      );
      const { appVersion } = promoteAppVersionFromDraftResponseSchema.parse(data);
      return { releasedVersion: appVersion.fullVersion ?? "" };
    } catch (cause) {
      throw new CliError({
        cause,
        code: CliErrorCode.FailedToReleaseDraftVersion()
      });
    }
  };
};

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/platform-links/resolve.ts
init_esm_shims();
var import_react2 = __toESM(require_react(), 1);
var ENVIRONMENT_COMMANDS = {
  development: "dev",
  preview: "preview",
  production: "release"
};
function getPlatformLinkCommand(environment) {
  return ENVIRONMENT_COMMANDS[environment];
}
function defaultLink(platform, url) {
  return { platform, label: DEFAULT_PLATFORM_LABELS[platform], url };
}
function resolvePlatformLinks(model, input, links) {
  if (model.pluginsInterface === void 0) {
    return links;
  }
  return model.pluginsInterface.resolvePlatformLinks({
    ...input,
    appId: model.config.appId,
    links
  });
}
function resolveSiteLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId, siteUrl }) {
  return resolvePlatformLinks(model, { cmd, overrideId, siteId }, [
    defaultLink("site", siteUrl),
    defaultLink("dashboard", getSiteDashboardUrl(siteId, overrideId)),
    defaultLink("editor", getSiteEditorUrl(siteId))
  ]);
}
function resolveAppLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId }) {
  const redirect = (platform) => createRedirectLink({
    platform,
    appId: model.config.appId,
    versionOverrideId: overrideId,
    siteId
  });
  return resolvePlatformLinks(model, { cmd, overrideId, siteId }, [
    defaultLink("site", redirect("site")),
    defaultLink("editor", redirect("editor")),
    defaultLink("dashboard", redirect("dashboard"))
  ]);
}
function useSiteLinks({
  cmd,
  siteId,
  overrideId,
  siteUrl
}) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react2.useMemo)(
    () => resolveSiteLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId, siteUrl }),
    [model, cmd, siteId, overrideId, siteUrl]
  );
}
function useAppLinks({
  cmd,
  siteId,
  overrideId
}) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react2.useMemo)(
    () => resolveAppLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId }),
    [model, cmd, siteId, overrideId]
  );
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/files/topology.ts
init_esm_shims();
async function readDeploymentTopology(projectFolder) {
  const path = getDeploymentTopologyPath(projectFolder);
  if (await pathExists(path)) {
    try {
      const deployment = await readJson(path);
      return deploymentTopologySchema.parse(deployment);
    } catch {
    }
  }
  return {};
}
async function writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(projectFolder, environment, entry) {
  const topology = await readDeploymentTopology(projectFolder);
  topology[environment] = entry;
  const path = getDeploymentTopologyPath(projectFolder);
  await writeJson(path, topology, { spaces: 2 });
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/hooks/useUpdateDeploymentTopology.ts
function toTopologyLink(links, platform) {
  const link = links.find((link2) => link2.platform === platform);
  if (link === void 0) {
    return;
  }
  return { main: link.url };
}
function useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react3.useCallback)(
    async ({
      environment,
      siteUrl,
      versionOverrideId
    }) => {
      const links = resolveSiteLinks(model, {
        cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId,
        siteUrl
      });
      await writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(
        model.projectFolder,
        environment,
        {
          id: versionOverrideId,
          site: toTopologyLink(links, "site"),
          dashboard: toTopologyLink(links, "dashboard")
        }
      );
    },
    [model, siteId]
  );
}
function useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForApp(siteId) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();

    async ({

    }) => {

        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId

          dashboard: toTopologyLink(links, "dashboard")

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/platform-links/resolve.ts
init_esm_shims();
var import_react2 = __toESM(require_react(), 1);
var ENVIRONMENT_COMMANDS = {
  development: "dev",
  preview: "preview",
  production: "release"
};
function getPlatformLinkCommand(environment) {
  return ENVIRONMENT_COMMANDS[environment];
}
function defaultLink(platform, url) {
  return { platform, label: DEFAULT_PLATFORM_LABELS[platform], url };
}
function resolvePlatformLinks(model, input, links) {
  if (model.pluginsInterface === void 0) {
    return links;
  }
  return model.pluginsInterface.resolvePlatformLinks({
    ...input,
    appId: model.config.appId,
    links
  });
}
function resolveSiteLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId, siteUrl }) {
  return resolvePlatformLinks(model, { cmd, overrideId, siteId }, [
    defaultLink("site", siteUrl),
    defaultLink("dashboard", getSiteDashboardUrl(siteId, overrideId)),
    defaultLink("editor", getSiteEditorUrl(siteId))
  ]);
}
function resolveAppLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId }) {
  const redirect = (platform) => createRedirectLink({
    platform,
    appId: model.config.appId,
    versionOverrideId: overrideId,
    siteId
  });
  return resolvePlatformLinks(model, { cmd, overrideId, siteId }, [
    defaultLink("site", redirect("site")),
    defaultLink("editor", redirect("editor")),
    defaultLink("dashboard", redirect("dashboard"))
  ]);
}
function useSiteLinks({
  cmd,
  siteId,
  overrideId,
  siteUrl
}) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react2.useMemo)(
    () => resolveSiteLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId, siteUrl }),
    [model, cmd, siteId, overrideId, siteUrl]
  );
}
function useAppLinks({
  cmd,
  siteId,
  overrideId
}) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react2.useMemo)(
    () => resolveAppLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId }),
    [model, cmd, siteId, overrideId]
  );
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/files/topology.ts
init_esm_shims();
async function readDeploymentTopology(projectFolder) {
  const path = getDeploymentTopologyPath(projectFolder);
  if (await pathExists(path)) {
    try {
      const deployment = await readJson(path);
      return deploymentTopologySchema.parse(deployment);
    } catch {
    }
  }
  return {};
}
async function writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(projectFolder, environment, entry) {
  const topology = await readDeploymentTopology(projectFolder);
  topology[environment] = entry;
  const path = getDeploymentTopologyPath(projectFolder);
  await writeJson(path, topology, { spaces: 2 });
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/hooks/useUpdateDeploymentTopology.ts
function toTopologyLink(links, platform) {
  const link = links.find((link2) => link2.platform === platform);
  if (link === void 0) {
    return;
  }
  return { main: link.url };
}
function useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react3.useCallback)(
    async ({
      environment,
      siteUrl,
      versionOverrideId
    }) => {
      const links = resolveSiteLinks(model, {
        cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId,
        siteUrl
      });
      await writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(
        model.projectFolder,
        environment,
        {
          id: versionOverrideId,
          site: toTopologyLink(links, "site"),
          dashboard: toTopologyLink(links, "dashboard")
        }
      );
    },
    [model, siteId]
  );
}
function useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForApp(siteId) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react3.useCallback)(
    async ({
      environment,
      versionOverrideId
    }) => {
      const links = resolveAppLinks(model, {
        cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId
      });

        model.projectFolder,
        environment,
        {
          id: versionOverrideId,
          site: toTopologyLink(links, "site"),
          editor: toTopologyLink(links, "editor"),
          dashboard: toTopologyLink(links, "dashboard")
        }
      );
    },
    [model, siteId]
  );
}

  return (0, import_react3.useCallback)(
    async ({
      environment,
      versionOverrideId
    }) => {
      const links = resolveAppLinks(model, {
        cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId

function resolveAppLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId }) {
  const redirect = (platform) => createRedirectLink({
    platform,
    appId: model.config.appId,
    versionOverrideId: overrideId,
    siteId
  });
  return resolvePlatformLinks(model, { cmd, overrideId, siteId }, [
    defaultLink("site", redirect("site")),
    defaultLink("editor", redirect("editor")),
    defaultLink("dashboard", redirect("dashboard"))
  ]);
}
function useSiteLinks({
  cmd,
  siteId,
  overrideId,
  siteUrl
}) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react2.useMemo)(
    () => resolveSiteLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId, siteUrl }),
    [model, cmd, siteId, overrideId, siteUrl]
  );
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/files/topology.ts
init_esm_shims();
async function readDeploymentTopology(projectFolder) {
  const path = getDeploymentTopologyPath(projectFolder);
  if (await pathExists(path)) {
    try {
      const deployment = await readJson(path);
      return deploymentTopologySchema.parse(deployment);
    } catch {
    }
  }
  return {};
}
async function writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(projectFolder, environment, entry) {
  const topology = await readDeploymentTopology(projectFolder);
  topology[environment] = entry;
  const path = getDeploymentTopologyPath(projectFolder);
  await writeJson(path, topology, { spaces: 2 });
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/hooks/useUpdateDeploymentTopology.ts
function toTopologyLink(links, platform) {
  const link = links.find((link2) => link2.platform === platform);
  if (link === void 0) {
    return;
  }
  return { main: link.url };
}

  siteId,
  overrideId,
  siteUrl
}) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react2.useMemo)(
    () => resolveSiteLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId, siteUrl }),
    [model, cmd, siteId, overrideId, siteUrl]
  );
}
function useAppLinks({
  cmd,
  siteId,
  overrideId
}) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react2.useMemo)(
    () => resolveAppLinks(model, { cmd, siteId, overrideId }),
    [model, cmd, siteId, overrideId]
  );
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/files/topology.ts
init_esm_shims();
async function readDeploymentTopology(projectFolder) {
  const path = getDeploymentTopologyPath(projectFolder);
  if (await pathExists(path)) {
    try {
      const deployment = await readJson(path);
      return deploymentTopologySchema.parse(deployment);
    } catch {
    }
  }
  return {};
}
async function writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(projectFolder, environment, entry) {
  const topology = await readDeploymentTopology(projectFolder);
  topology[environment] = entry;
  const path = getDeploymentTopologyPath(projectFolder);
  await writeJson(path, topology, { spaces: 2 });
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/hooks/useUpdateDeploymentTopology.ts
function toTopologyLink(links, platform) {
  const link = links.find((link2) => link2.platform === platform);
  if (link === void 0) {
    return;
  }
  return { main: link.url };
}
function useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react3.useCallback)(
    async ({
      environment,
      siteUrl,
      versionOverrideId
    }) => {
      const links = resolveSiteLinks(model, {
        cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId,
        siteUrl
      });
      await writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(
        model.projectFolder,
        environment,
        {
          id: versionOverrideId,
          site: toTopologyLink(links, "site"),
          dashboard: toTopologyLink(links, "dashboard")
        }
      );
    },
    [model, siteId]
  );
}
function useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForApp(siteId) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react3.useCallback)(
    async ({
      environment,
      versionOverrideId
    }) => {
      const links = resolveAppLinks(model, {
        cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId
      });
      await writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(
        model.projectFolder,
        environment,
        {
          id: versionOverrideId,
          site: toTopologyLink(links, "site"),
          editor: toTopologyLink(links, "editor"),
          dashboard: toTopologyLink(links, "dashboard")
        }
      );
    },
    [model, siteId]
  );
}

      version: external_exports.string().max(255).min(1)
    }).optional()
  }).optional()
});

  iframeUrl: external_exports.string().optional(),
  scriptAsset: external_exports.object({
    exportedName: external_exports.string(),
    containerId: external_exports.string(),
    url: external_exports.string(),
    scriptType: external_exports.string().optional()
  }).optional()
});
var backendWorkerSchema = external_exports.object({
  compType: external_exports.literal(ComponentType.BACKEND_WORKER),
  compId: external_exports.string(),
  compName: external_exports.string().optional(),
  createdBy: external_exports.string().optional(),
  createdByVersion: external_exports.string().optional(),
  compData: external_exports.object({
    backendWorker: external_exports.object({
      deploymentId: external_exports.string().uuid(),
      deploymentUrl: external_exports.string().url().optional(),
      label: external_exports.string().max(200).optional(),
      commitHash: external_exports.string().max(40).optional()
    })
  })
});
var aiContextSchema = external_exports.object({
  description: external_exports.string().min(20).max(1e4)
}).nullish();
var backofficePageCompDataSchema = external_exports.object({
  hostingPlatform: external_exports.string(),
  fullPage: external_exports.boolean().nullish(),
  hideInSidebar: external_exports.boolean().nullish(),
  mobileCompatible: external_exports.boolean().nullish(),

}).merge(errorReportingSchema).merge(componentSourceSchema).passthrough();
var widgetCompDataSchema = external_exports.object({
  width: external_exports.number().nullish(),
  height: external_exports.number().nullish()
}).merge(componentSourceSchema).passthrough();

}).passthrough().optional();

  compData: external_exports.object({
    backOfficePage: backofficePageCompDataSchema
  }),
  compName: external_exports.string().optional(),

}).passthrough();

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/files/topology.ts
init_esm_shims();
async function readDeploymentTopology(projectFolder) {
  const path = getDeploymentTopologyPath(projectFolder);
  if (await pathExists(path)) {
    try {
      const deployment = await readJson(path);
      return deploymentTopologySchema.parse(deployment);
    } catch {
    }
  }
  return {};
}
async function writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(projectFolder, environment, entry) {
  const topology = await readDeploymentTopology(projectFolder);
  topology[environment] = entry;
  const path = getDeploymentTopologyPath(projectFolder);
  await writeJson(path, topology, { spaces: 2 });
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/hooks/useUpdateDeploymentTopology.ts
function toTopologyLink(links, platform) {
  const link = links.find((link2) => link2.platform === platform);
  if (link === void 0) {
    return;
  }
  return { main: link.url };
}
function useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react3.useCallback)(
    async ({
      environment,
      siteUrl,
      versionOverrideId
    }) => {
      const links = resolveSiteLinks(model, {
        cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId,
        siteUrl
      });
      await writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(
        model.projectFolder,
        environment,
        {
          id: versionOverrideId,
          site: toTopologyLink(links, "site"),
          dashboard: toTopologyLink(links, "dashboard")
        }
      );
    },
    [model, siteId]
  );
}
function useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForApp(siteId) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return (0, import_react3.useCallback)(
    async ({
      environment,
      versionOverrideId
    }) => {
      const links = resolveAppLinks(model, {
        cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
        siteId,
        overrideId: versionOverrideId
      });
      await writeDeploymentTopologyEnvironment(

        environment,
        {
          id: versionOverrideId,
          site: toTopologyLink(links, "site"),
          editor: toTopologyLink(links, "editor"),
          dashboard: toTopologyLink(links, "dashboard")
        }
      );
    },
    [model, siteId]
  );
}

// ../cli-app-installer/src/app-marker-url.ts
init_esm_shims();
function getAppMarketUrl({
  appId,
  siteId,
  version,
  versionOverrideId,
  postInstallationUrl
}) {
  const installerUrl = new URL("https://www.wix.com/app-installer");
  installerUrl.searchParams.set("appId", appId);
  if (siteId) {
    installerUrl.searchParams.set("metaSiteId", siteId);
  }
  if (version) {
    installerUrl.searchParams.set("version", version);
  }
  if (versionOverrideId) {
    installerUrl.searchParams.set("versionOverrideId", versionOverrideId);
  }
  installerUrl.searchParams.set("postInstallationUrl", postInstallationUrl);
  return installerUrl.toString();
}

// ../cli-app-installer/src/redirect-service-url.ts
init_esm_shims();
function getRedirectServiceUrl({
  platform,
  siteId,
  appId,
  versionOverrideId
}) {
  const redirectUrl = new URL(
    "https://dev.wix.com/wix-cli-redirect-service/redirect"
  );
  redirectUrl.searchParams.set("platform", platform);
  if (versionOverrideId) {
    redirectUrl.searchParams.set("versionOverrideId", versionOverrideId);
  }
  if (siteId) {
    redirectUrl.searchParams.set("tenantId", siteId);
  }
  if (appId) {
    redirectUrl.searchParams.set("appId", appId);
  }
  return redirectUrl.toString();
}
