// ../cli-app-installer/src/useWebSocketServer.ts
init_esm_shims();

// ../cli-app-installer-comm-api/build/index.js
init_esm_shims();
init_esm();
import { createServer as createHttpServer } from "node:http";
function createAppInstallerServer() {
  let isRunning = false;
  const server = createHttpServer();
  const io = new Server(
    server,
    { cors: { origin: "https://dev.wix.com", methods: ["GET", "POST"] } }
  );
  const callbacksEmitter = new Emitter();
  return {
    async serve() {
      if (isRunning) {
        throw new Error("Server is already running");
      }
      io.on("connect", (socket) => {
        socket.on("init", (payload, callback) => {
          callbacksEmitter.emit("init", payload);
          callback();
        });
      });
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        server.once("error", reject);
        server.listen(0, () => {
          isRunning = true;
          server.removeListener("error", reject);
          const { port } = server.address();
          resolve({ port });
        });
      });
    },
    onConnect(handler) {
      callbacksEmitter.on("init", handler);
      return () => {
        callbacksEmitter.removeListener("init", handler);
      };
    },
    close() {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        if (!isRunning) {
          resolve();
        } else {
          io.close((err) => {
            isRunning = false;
            callbacksEmitter.removeAllListeners();
            if (err) {
              reject(err);
            } else {
              resolve();
            }
          }).catch(reject);
        }
      });
    }
  };
}

// ../cli-app-installer/src/useWebSocketServer.ts
function useWebSocketServer(onError) {
  const { execute } = useAsyncCallback(
    async ({ signal }) => {
      const server = createAppInstallerServer();
      signal.onabort = () => {
        server.close().catch(onError);
      };
      return server;
    },
    { onError }
  );
  return execute;
}

  const createWsServer = useWebSocketServer(onError);

function getAppMarketUrl({
  appId,
  siteId,
  version,
  versionOverrideId,
  postInstallationUrl
}) {
  const installerUrl = new URL("https://www.wix.com/app-installer");
  installerUrl.searchParams.set("appId", appId);
  if (siteId) {
    installerUrl.searchParams.set("metaSiteId", siteId);
  }
  if (version) {
    installerUrl.searchParams.set("version", version);
  }
  if (versionOverrideId) {
    installerUrl.searchParams.set("versionOverrideId", versionOverrideId);
  }
  installerUrl.searchParams.set("postInstallationUrl", postInstallationUrl);
  return installerUrl.toString();
}

// ../cli-app-installer/src/redirect-service-url.ts
init_esm_shims();
function getRedirectServiceUrl({
  platform,
  siteId,
  appId,
  versionOverrideId
}) {
  const redirectUrl = new URL(
    "https://dev.wix.com/wix-cli-redirect-service/redirect"
  );
  redirectUrl.searchParams.set("platform", platform);
  if (versionOverrideId) {
    redirectUrl.searchParams.set("versionOverrideId", versionOverrideId);
  }
  if (siteId) {
    redirectUrl.searchParams.set("tenantId", siteId);
  }
  if (appId) {
    redirectUrl.searchParams.set("appId", appId);
  }
  return redirectUrl.toString();
}

// ../cli-app-installer/src/app-installer-url.ts
function getAppInstallerUrl({
  wsPort,
  appId
}) {
  const postInstallationUrl = new URL(
    "https://dev.wix.com/wix-cli/app-installation-success/"
  );
  postInstallationUrl.searchParams.set("wsPort", wsPort.toString());
  return getAppMarketUrl({
    appId,
    version: "dev",
    postInstallationUrl: postInstallationUrl.toString()
  });
}

  appId,

  return getAppMarketUrl({

    version: "dev",
    postInstallationUrl: redirectUrl.toString()
  });
}

// ../cli-app-installer-comm-api/build/index.js
init_esm_shims();
init_esm();
import { createServer as createHttpServer } from "node:http";
function createAppInstallerServer() {
  let isRunning = false;
  const server = createHttpServer();
  const io = new Server(
    server,
    { cors: { origin: "https://dev.wix.com", methods: ["GET", "POST"] } }
  );
  const callbacksEmitter = new Emitter();
  return {
    async serve() {
      if (isRunning) {
        throw new Error("Server is already running");
      }
      io.on("connect", (socket) => {
        socket.on("init", (payload, callback) => {
          callbacksEmitter.emit("init", payload);
          callback();
        });
      });
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        server.once("error", reject);
        server.listen(0, () => {
          isRunning = true;
          server.removeListener("error", reject);
          const { port } = server.address();
          resolve({ port });
        });
      });
    },
    onConnect(handler) {
      callbacksEmitter.on("init", handler);
      return () => {
        callbacksEmitter.removeListener("init", handler);
      };
    },
    close() {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        if (!isRunning) {
          resolve();
        } else {
          io.close((err) => {
            isRunning = false;
            callbacksEmitter.removeAllListeners();
            if (err) {
              reject(err);
            } else {
              resolve();
            }
          }).catch(reject);
        }
      });
    }
  };
}

// ../cli-app-installer/src/useWebSocketServer.ts
function useWebSocketServer(onError) {
  const { execute } = useAsyncCallback(
    async ({ signal }) => {
      const server = createAppInstallerServer();
      signal.onabort = () => {
        server.close().catch(onError);
      };
      return server;
    },
    { onError }
  );
  return execute;
}

// ../cli-app-installer/src/AppInstaller.tsx
var AppInstaller = ({ appId, onSubmit, onError }) => {
  const createWsServer = useWebSocketServer(onError);
  const { status } = useAsync(
    async () => {
      const wsServer = await createWsServer();
      const { port } = await wsServer.serve();
      wsServer.onConnect(async ({ siteId }) => {
        onSubmit(siteId);
      });
      return getAppInstallerUrl({ wsPort: port, appId });
    },
    [],
    { onError }
  );

// ../cli-app-installer-comm-api/build/index.js
init_esm_shims();
init_esm();
import { createServer as createHttpServer } from "node:http";
function createAppInstallerServer() {
  let isRunning = false;
  const server = createHttpServer();
  const io = new Server(
    server,
    { cors: { origin: "https://dev.wix.com", methods: ["GET", "POST"] } }
  );
  const callbacksEmitter = new Emitter();
  return {
    async serve() {
      if (isRunning) {
        throw new Error("Server is already running");
      }
      io.on("connect", (socket) => {
        socket.on("init", (payload, callback) => {
          callbacksEmitter.emit("init", payload);
          callback();
        });
      });
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        server.once("error", reject);
        server.listen(0, () => {
          isRunning = true;
          server.removeListener("error", reject);
          const { port } = server.address();
          resolve({ port });
        });
      });
    },
    onConnect(handler) {
      callbacksEmitter.on("init", handler);
      return () => {
        callbacksEmitter.removeListener("init", handler);
      };
    },
    close() {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        if (!isRunning) {
          resolve();
        } else {
          io.close((err) => {
            isRunning = false;
            callbacksEmitter.removeAllListeners();
            if (err) {
              reject(err);
            } else {
              resolve();
            }
          }).catch(reject);
        }
      });
    }
  };
}

// ../cli-app-installer/src/useWebSocketServer.ts
function useWebSocketServer(onError) {
  const { execute } = useAsyncCallback(
    async ({ signal }) => {
      const server = createAppInstallerServer();
      signal.onabort = () => {
        server.close().catch(onError);
      };
      return server;
    },
    { onError }
  );
  return execute;
}

// ../cli-app-installer/src/AppInstaller.tsx
var AppInstaller = ({ appId, onSubmit, onError }) => {
  const createWsServer = useWebSocketServer(onError);
  const { status } = useAsync(
    async () => {
      const wsServer = await createWsServer();
      const { port } = await wsServer.serve();
      wsServer.onConnect(async ({ siteId }) => {
        onSubmit(siteId);
      });
      return getAppInstallerUrl({ wsPort: port, appId });
    },
    [],
    { onError }
  );
  return (0, import_variant.match)(status, {
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(Spinner, { text: "Loading..." }),
    Success: ({ result: appInstallerUrl }) => /* @__PURE__ */ import_react2.default.createElement(OpenBrowser, { url: appInstallerUrl })
  });
};

  for (const uploadBucket of uploadBuckets) {
    if (!uploadBucket.hashes || uploadBucket.hashes.length === 0) {
      continue;
    }
    const form = new FormData();
    await Promise.all(
      uploadBucket.hashes.map(async (hash) => {
        const staticFileMetadata = staticFilesMetadataByHash.get(hash);
        if (!staticFileMetadata) {

          }),
          hash
        );
      })
    );
    batches.push(form);
  }
  return batches;
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/PreviewCommand/hooks/useCreatePreview.ts
var DeploymentTarget = (0, import_variant2.variant)({
  Site: (0, import_variant2.fields)(),
  App: (0, import_variant2.fields)()
});
function useCreatePreview({
  baseUrl,
  label,
  previewId: existingPreviewId,
  onPreviewCreated
}) {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  const devCenterOverrides = useDevCenterOverridesClient();
  const loadBuildFiles = useLoadBuildFiles();
  const deployFiles = useDeployFiles();
  const { updateManifestWithBackendWorker } = useUpdateManifest();
  const uploadApplicationAction = useAsyncCallback(async () => {
    const buildFiles = await loadBuildFiles.execute();
    if (buildFiles.client.length + buildFiles.server.length === 0) {
      if (model.config.projectType === "Site") {
        throw new CliError({
          code: CliErrorCode.BuildOutputMissing({
            reason: "Couldn't find any client or server build output files."
          }),
          cause: null
        });
      }
      return DeploymentTarget.App({});
    }
    const { deploymentBaseUrl, deploymentId } = await deployFiles.execute(buildFiles);
    return DeploymentTarget[model.config.projectType]({
      deploymentUrl: deploymentBaseUrl,
      deploymentId
    });
  });
  const createPreviewIdAction = useAsyncCallback(
    async (_, serverUrl, deploymentId) => {
      const gitCommitHash = await isInsideGitRepo(model.projectFolder) ? await getGitLatestCommitHash(model.projectFolder) : void 0;
      const { appManifest: rawManifest, componentPlaceholderFieldMasks } = await processAppManifest({
        projectFolder: model.projectFolder,
        config: model.config,
        serverUrl
      });
      const appManifest = deploymentId && serverUrl ? updateManifestWithBackendWorker(
        rawManifest,
        deploymentId,
        serverUrl,
        label,
        gitCommitHash
      ) : rawManifest;
      let previewId;
      if (existingPreviewId) {
        previewId = await devCenterOverrides.updateOverrideWithNoSideEffects({
          id: existingPreviewId,
          appManifest,
          componentFieldMasks: componentPlaceholderFieldMasks,
          projectId: model.releaseAppId
        });
      } else {
        previewId = await devCenterOverrides.createComponentsOverride({
          appManifest,
          projectId: model.releaseAppId,
          experiments: toComponentExperiments(appManifest.components)
        });
      }
      return { previewId, appManifest };
    }
  );
  const { status } = useAsync(async () => {
    await assertBuildOutputExists(model.projectFolder, model.config);
    const deployment = baseUrl ? DeploymentTarget[model.config.projectType]({ deploymentUrl: baseUrl }) : await uploadApplicationAction.execute();
    const { deploymentUrl, deploymentId } = deployment;
    const { previewId, appManifest } = await createPreviewIdAction.execute(
      deploymentUrl,
      deploymentId
    );
    const result = { deployment, previewId, appManifest };
    onPreviewCreated?.(result);
    return result;
  }, []);
  return {
    uploadStatus: uploadApplicationAction.status,
    createPreviewStatus: createPreviewIdAction.status,
    status
  };
}

var SiteTopologyAndLinks = ({ environment, previewId, previewUrl, onTopologyUpdated }) => {
  const { appId, siteId } = useSiteConfig();
  const updateDeploymentTopology = useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId);
  const veloClient = useVeloClient();

    cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
    siteId,
    overrideId: previewId,
    siteUrl: previewUrl
  });

    const [, gitHubOnboarding] = await Promise.all([
      updateDeploymentTopology({
        siteUrl: previewUrl,
        versionOverrideId: previewId,
        environment
      }),
      veloClient.getGitHubOnboardingStatus(appId)
    ]);
    onTopologyUpdated();
    return { gitHubOnboarding };
  }, []);
  return /* @__PURE__ */ import_react3.default.createElement(import_react3.default.Fragment, null, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react3.default.createElement(
    ActionStatus,
    {
      status,
      loadingText: "Updating deployment topology manifest...",
      successText: "Deployment topology manifest updated successfully!"
    }
  ), (0, import_variant3.match)(status, {
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => null,
    Success: ({ result: { gitHubOnboarding } }) => {
      const isGitHubEnabled = gitHubOnboarding?.status === OnboardingState.GITHUB_ENABLED;
      const visibleLinks = isGitHubEnabled ? links : links.filter((link) => link.platform !== "editor");
      return /* @__PURE__ */ import_react3.default.createElement(PlatformLinksSection, { links: visibleLinks });
    }
  }));
};

var PreviewCommand = ({ environment, baseUrl, label, previewId, onPreviewCreated }) => {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return model.config.projectType === "Site" ? /* @__PURE__ */ import_react6.default.createElement(SiteAuthProvider, { siteId: model.config.siteId }, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react6.default.createElement(
    Preview,
    {
      baseUrl,
      previewId,
      onPreviewCreated,
      environment,
      label
    }
  )) : /* @__PURE__ */ import_react6.default.createElement(

=====

FILE /tmp/wixcli/node_modules/@wix/cli/build/chunk-RPYUT3H2.js LINE 1683
  const loadBuildFiles = useLoadBuildFiles();
  const deployFiles = useDeployFiles();
  const { updateManifestWithBackendWorker } = useUpdateManifest();
  const uploadApplicationAction = useAsyncCallback(async () => {
    const buildFiles = await loadBuildFiles.execute();
    if (buildFiles.client.length + buildFiles.server.length === 0) {
      if (model.config.projectType === "Site") {
        throw new CliError({
          code: CliErrorCode.BuildOutputMissing({
            reason: "Couldn't find any client or server build output files."
          }),
          cause: null
        });
      }
      return DeploymentTarget.App({});
    }
    const { deploymentBaseUrl, deploymentId } = await deployFiles.execute(buildFiles);
    return DeploymentTarget[model.config.projectType]({
      deploymentUrl: deploymentBaseUrl,
      deploymentId
    });
  });
  const createPreviewIdAction = useAsyncCallback(
    async (_, serverUrl, deploymentId) => {
      const gitCommitHash = await isInsideGitRepo(model.projectFolder) ? await getGitLatestCommitHash(model.projectFolder) : void 0;
      const { appManifest: rawManifest, componentPlaceholderFieldMasks } = await processAppManifest({
        projectFolder: model.projectFolder,
        config: model.config,
        serverUrl
      });
      const appManifest = deploymentId && serverUrl ? updateManifestWithBackendWorker(
        rawManifest,
        deploymentId,
        serverUrl,
        label,
        gitCommitHash
      ) : rawManifest;
      let previewId;
      if (existingPreviewId) {
        previewId = await devCenterOverrides.updateOverrideWithNoSideEffects({
          id: existingPreviewId,
          appManifest,
          componentFieldMasks: componentPlaceholderFieldMasks,
          projectId: model.releaseAppId
        });
      } else {
        previewId = await devCenterOverrides.createComponentsOverride({
          appManifest,
          projectId: model.releaseAppId,
          experiments: toComponentExperiments(appManifest.components)
        });
      }
      return { previewId, appManifest };
    }
  );
  const { status } = useAsync(async () => {
    await assertBuildOutputExists(model.projectFolder, model.config);
    const deployment = baseUrl ? DeploymentTarget[model.config.projectType]({ deploymentUrl: baseUrl }) : await uploadApplicationAction.execute();
    const { deploymentUrl, deploymentId } = deployment;
    const { previewId, appManifest } = await createPreviewIdAction.execute(
      deploymentUrl,
      deploymentId
    );
    const result = { deployment, previewId, appManifest };
    onPreviewCreated?.(result);
    return result;
  }, []);
  return {
    uploadStatus: uploadApplicationAction.status,
    createPreviewStatus: createPreviewIdAction.status,
    status
  };
}

// ../cli-astro-commands/src/components/PreviewCommand/SiteTopologyAndLinks.tsx
init_esm_shims();
var import_react3 = __toESM(require_react(), 1);
var import_variant3 = __toESM(require_lib(), 1);
var SiteTopologyAndLinks = ({ environment, previewId, previewUrl, onTopologyUpdated }) => {
  const { appId, siteId } = useSiteConfig();
  const updateDeploymentTopology = useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId);
  const veloClient = useVeloClient();
  const links = useSiteLinks({
    cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
    siteId,
    overrideId: previewId,
    siteUrl: previewUrl
  });
  const { status } = useAsync(async () => {
    const [, gitHubOnboarding] = await Promise.all([
      updateDeploymentTopology({
        siteUrl: previewUrl,
        versionOverrideId: previewId,
        environment
      }),
      veloClient.getGitHubOnboardingStatus(appId)
    ]);
    onTopologyUpdated();
    return { gitHubOnboarding };
  }, []);
  return /* @__PURE__ */ import_react3.default.createElement(import_react3.default.Fragment, null, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react3.default.createElement(
    ActionStatus,
    {
      status,
      loadingText: "Updating deployment topology manifest...",
      successText: "Deployment topology manifest updated successfully!"
    }
  ), (0, import_variant3.match)(status, {
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => null,
    Success: ({ result: { gitHubOnboarding } }) => {
      const isGitHubEnabled = gitHubOnboarding?.status === OnboardingState.GITHUB_ENABLED;
      const visibleLinks = isGitHubEnabled ? links : links.filter((link) => link.platform !== "editor");
      return /* @__PURE__ */ import_react3.default.createElement(PlatformLinksSection, { links: visibleLinks });
    }
  }));
};

var PreviewCommand = ({ environment, baseUrl, label, previewId, onPreviewCreated }) => {
  const { model } = useProjectModel();
  return model.config.projectType === "Site" ? /* @__PURE__ */ import_react6.default.createElement(SiteAuthProvider, { siteId: model.config.siteId }, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react6.default.createElement(
    Preview,
    {
      baseUrl,
      previewId,
      onPreviewCreated,
      environment,
      label
    }
  )) : /* @__PURE__ */ import_react6.default.createElement(

    {

      previewId,

      environment,
      label
    }

//# sourceMappingURL=chunk-RPYUT3H2.js.map

=====

FILE /tmp/wixcli/node_modules/@wix/cli/build/chunk-RPYUT3H2.js LINE 1694
          cause: null
        });
      }
      return DeploymentTarget.App({});
    }
    const { deploymentBaseUrl, deploymentId } = await deployFiles.execute(buildFiles);
    return DeploymentTarget[model.config.projectType]({
      deploymentUrl: deploymentBaseUrl,
      deploymentId
    });
  });
  const createPreviewIdAction = useAsyncCallback(
    async (_, serverUrl, deploymentId) => {
      const gitCommitHash = await isInsideGitRepo(model.projectFolder) ? await getGitLatestCommitHash(model.projectFolder) : void 0;
      const { appManifest: rawManifest, componentPlaceholderFieldMasks } = await processAppManifest({
        projectFolder: model.projectFolder,
        config: model.config,
        serverUrl
      });
      const appManifest = deploymentId && serverUrl ? updateManifestWithBackendWorker(
        rawManifest,
        deploymentId,
        serverUrl,
        label,
        gitCommitHash
      ) : rawManifest;
      let previewId;
      if (existingPreviewId) {
        previewId = await devCenterOverrides.updateOverrideWithNoSideEffects({
          id: existingPreviewId,
          appManifest,
          componentFieldMasks: componentPlaceholderFieldMasks,
          projectId: model.releaseAppId
        });
      } else {
        previewId = await devCenterOverrides.createComponentsOverride({
          appManifest,
          projectId: model.releaseAppId,
          experiments: toComponentExperiments(appManifest.components)
        });
      }
      return { previewId, appManifest };
    }
  );
  const { status } = useAsync(async () => {
    await assertBuildOutputExists(model.projectFolder, model.config);
    const deployment = baseUrl ? DeploymentTarget[model.config.projectType]({ deploymentUrl: baseUrl }) : await uploadApplicationAction.execute();
    const { deploymentUrl, deploymentId } = deployment;
    const { previewId, appManifest } = await createPreviewIdAction.execute(
      deploymentUrl,
      deploymentId
    );
    const result = { deployment, previewId, appManifest };
    onPreviewCreated?.(result);
    return result;
  }, []);
  return {
    uploadStatus: uploadApplicationAction.status,
    createPreviewStatus: createPreviewIdAction.status,
    status
  };
}

init_esm_shims();
var import_react3 = __toESM(require_react(), 1);
var import_variant3 = __toESM(require_lib(), 1);
var SiteTopologyAndLinks = ({ environment, previewId, previewUrl, onTopologyUpdated }) => {
  const { appId, siteId } = useSiteConfig();
  const updateDeploymentTopology = useUpdateDeploymentTopologyForSite(siteId);
  const veloClient = useVeloClient();
  const links = useSiteLinks({
    cmd: getPlatformLinkCommand(environment),
    siteId,
    overrideId: previewId,
    siteUrl: previewUrl
  });
  const { status } = useAsync(async () => {
    const [, gitHubOnboarding] = await Promise.all([
      updateDeploymentTopology({
        siteUrl: previewUrl,
        versionOverrideId: previewId,
        environment
      }),
      veloClient.getGitHubOnboardingStatus(appId)
    ]);
    onTopologyUpdated();
    return { gitHubOnboarding };
  }, []);
  return /* @__PURE__ */ import_react3.default.createElement(import_react3.default.Fragment, null, /* @__PURE__ */ import_react3.default.createElement(
    ActionStatus,
    {
      status,
      loadingText: "Updating deployment topology manifest...",
      successText: "Deployment topology manifest updated successfully!"
    }
  ), (0, import_variant3.match)(status, {
    Error: () => null,
    Loading: () => null,
    Success: ({ result: { gitHubOnboarding } }) => {
      const isGitHubEnabled = gitHubOnboarding?.status === OnboardingState.GITHUB_ENABLED;
      const visibleLinks = isGitHubEnabled ? links : links.filter((link) => link.platform !== "editor");
      return /* @__PURE__ */ import_react3.default.createElement(PlatformLinksSection, { links: visibleLinks });
    }
  }));
};

  const logPipelineErrorInformation = ({
    pipelinesDescription
  }) => {
    const errorBadge = t("publish_command.publish_pipeline_error_badge");
    const taskDone = source_default.green(t("publish_command.generic_task_done"));
    const taskFailed = source_default.red(t("publish_command.generic_task_failed"));
    const divider = "-".repeat(30);
    const padding = "  ";
    const normalizeText = (text) => {
      return text.split("\n").map((line, index) => {
        if (line.trim() && index) {
          return `${padding}${line}`;
        }
        return line;
      }).join("\n");
    };
    let output = "";
    output += `
${source_default.red(errorBadge)} ${t(
      "publish_command.publish_pipeline_error_title"
    )}
`;
    output += `
${divider}
`;
    output += `Status: ${taskFailed}`;
    const tasksDescriptions = pipelinesDescription[0]?.tasksDescriptions;
    if (tasksDescriptions) {
      for (const task of tasksDescriptions) {
        output += `

* ${task.taskName}... ${task.status === "ERROR" ? `${taskFailed}:` : taskDone}
`;
        if (task.description) {
          const errorDescription = JSON.parse(task.description);
          for (const fileName in errorDescription) {
            output += `
${padding}${errorBadge} ${fileName} | `;
            output += normalizeText(errorDescription[fileName].toString());
          }
        }
      }
    }
    output += `
${divider}`;
    logger.log(output);
  };
  const logPublishing = () => {
    const loader = logger.logWithLoader({
      text: t("publish_command.publishing")
    });
    return {
      success: ({ siteUrl }) => {
        loader.succeed(
          t("publish_command.published", {
            url: source_default.underline(source_default.blue(siteUrl))
          })
        );
      },
      fail: () => loader.stop()
    };
  };
  const logDeploying = () => {
    const loader = logger.logWithLoader({
      text: t("publish_command.deploying")
    });

      success: () => {
        loader.succeed(
          `${t("publish_command.deploying")} ${source_default.green(
            t("publish_command.generic_task_done")
          )}`
        );
      },
      fail: () => loader.fail(
        `${t("publish_command.deploying")} ${source_default.red(
          t("publish_command.generic_task_failed")
        )}`
      )
    };
  };
  const logForceDeployWarning = () => logger.log(
    source_default.red(
      `${source_default.bold(
        t("publish_command.publish_with_errors_caution_title")

    )

${t("publish_command.publish_aborted")}`);
  const logCreatingPreview = () => logger.log(`
${t("preview_command.creating_preview")}
`);
  const logPreviewCreated = (url) => logger.log(t("preview_command.preview_created", { url: source_default.green(url) }));
  const logPreviewUnsupported = () => logger.log(t("preview_command.preview_unsupported"));
  const logFailedToGetLatestRevision = (error) => {
    const cause = error instanceof Error ? error.stack : error;
    logger.log(t("publish_command.failed_getting_latest_revision", { cause }));
  };
  const logMigratingToAnyNpm = () => {

    );
  };
  const logMigrationToAnyNpmCompletedSuccessfully = () => logger.log(source_default.green(t("install_command.migration_to_any_npm_success")));
  const logMigrationToAnyNpmFailed = () => {
    logger.log(source_default.red(t("install_command.migration_to_any_npm_failed")));
  };
  const logPreviewing = () => {
    const loader = logger.logWithLoader({
      text: t("publish_command.creating_preview")
    });
    return {
      success: () => loader.succeed(t("publish_command.creating_preview_success")),
      fail: () => loader.fail(t("publish_command.creating_preview_failed")),
      stop: () => loader.stop()
    };
  };
  return {
    t,
    logAborting,
    logSyncingLocalCode,
    logRecoverableFailureToRemoveTypesDir,
    logUpdatedLocalCodeToRevision,
    logSyncingPagesToRevision,

    });

        }
      } catch (err) {
        throw new CliError({
          code: CliErrorCode.FailedToGetDeploymentPipelineDescription(),
          cause: err
        });
      }
      logger.logForceDeployWarning();
      const shouldSkipPipelineCheck = options.force ? { value: true } : await (0, import_prompts.default)({
        type: "confirm",
        name: "value",
        message: t("publish_command.prompt_publish_with_error"),
        initial: true
      });
      skipPipelineCheck = shouldSkipPipelineCheck.value;
    }
    if (deploymentStatus === DeploymentPipelinesStatus.SUCCESS || skipPipelineCheck) {
      const logPublished = logger.logPublishing();
      try {
        await httpRequest(
          {
            type: "editor",
            authState
          },
          publishDeployment({
            deploymentId,
            skipPipelineCheck
          }),
          { retries: 2 }
        );
      } catch (err) {
        logPublished.fail();
        throw new CliError({
          code: CliErrorCode.FailedToPublishDeployment(),
          cause: err
        });
      }
      logPublished.success({
        siteUrl: toBaseSiteUrl(deploymentUrl)
      });
    }
  } else {
    logger.logPublishAborted();
  }
}
export {
  publish
};
